<cot/>\n            </task>\n            <doc>\n            \n以下是目标中间表示的规范文档：\n# XCPG-IR 规范 v1.0\n## 1. 什么是 CPG\nCPG = AST + CFG + DFG 的融合图。\n 它是静态分析工具（如 Joern、CodeQL）的底层语义模型。\n### 2.1 节点类型（Nodes）\n| 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. This could lead to SQL injection if variables in the SQL statement are not properly sanitized. <cot/>\n            </task>\n            <doc>\n            \n以下是目标中间表示的规范文档：\n# XCPG-IR 规范 v1.0\n## 1. 什么是 CPG\nCPG = AST + CFG + DFG 的融合图。\n 它是静态分析工具（如 Joern、CodeQL）的底层语义模型。\n### 2.1 节点类型（Nodes）\n| 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. <cot/>\n            </task>\n            <doc>\n            \n以下是目标中间表示的规范文档：\n# XCPG-IR 规范 v1.0\n## 1. 什么是 CPG\nCPG = AST + CFG + DFG 的融合图。\n 它是静态分析工具（如 Joern、CodeQL）的底层语义模型。\n### 2.1 节点类型（Nodes）\n| 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object. 类别     | Node Kind                                                    | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ---- |\n| 程序结构 | `FILE`, `NAMESPACE_BLOCK`, `TYPE_DECL`, `METHOD`, `BLOCK`    |      |\n| 参数变量 | `METHOD_PARAMETER_IN`, `METHOD_RETURN`, `LOCAL`, `MEMBER`    |      |\n| 表达式   | `CALL`, `IDENTIFIER`, `LITERAL`, `FIELD_IDENTIFIER`, `ASSIGNMENT`, `RETURN` |      |\n| 控制流   | `CONTROL_STRUCTURE`, `JUMP_TARGET`                           |      |\n| 类型系统 | `TYPE`, `TYPE_ARGUMENT`                                      |      |\n| 模块化   | `IMPORT`, `PACKAGE`                                          |      |\n### 2.2 边类型（Edges）\n| 类别     | Edge Kind                             | 说明 |\n| -------- | ------------------------------------- | ---- |\n| 语法     | `AST`, `ARGUMENT`, `RECEIVER`         |      |\n| 控制流   | `CFG`, `CDG`, `DOMINATE`              |      |\n| 数据流   | `DFG`, `REACHING_DEF`, `PHI`, `ALIAS` |      |\n| 调用图   | `CALL`, `REF`, `BINDS_TO`             |      |\n| 类型关系 | `EVAL_TYPE`, `INHERITS_FROM`          |      |\n| 特殊     | `CAPTURED_BY`（闭包捕获）             |      |\n👉 意义：\n- Source/Sink 对应 CPG 节点；\n- 传播 Propagation 对应 CPG 边；\n------\n## 2. 什么是 XCPG\nXCPG = eXtended CPG，在原始 CPG 的基础上扩展：\n- 引入 跨语言统一 API 签名；\n- 定义 污点标签体系 (taint kinds)；\n- 明确 Sanitizer（净化）与 Barrier（阻断）；\n- 增加 Flow constraints（must/must_not）用于复杂路径约束；\n- 提供可迁移到 CodeQL / Semgrep / PMD / GoSec 的抽象层。\n------\n## 4. XCPG-IR 结构\n```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: string\n  kind: taint | pattern | hybrid | value\n  severity: INFO | WARNING | ERROR | CRITICAL\n  message: string\nsources: [...]\nsinks: [...]\nsanitizers: [...]\npropagations: [...]\n```\n------\n## 5. Match（节点匹配）\n所有实体（Source / Sink / Sanitizer / Barrier）都通过 match 来定位 CPG 节点。\n```yaml\nmatch:\n  node: CALL | LITERAL | PARAM | RETURN | FIELD | METHOD\n  api:\n    package: string\n    type: string\n    method: string\n    signature: [\"(...)\"]\n  type:\n    name: string\n    match: exact | subtype\n  arg_index: int\n  where:\n    - op: eq | neq | regex | in\n      left: 属性名 (value, methodFullName, arg[0].value)\n      right: 值\n```\n------\n## 6. 实体定义\n### Source\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - id: USER_INPUT\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: javax.servlet.ServletRequest, method: getParameter }\n```\n### Sink\n```yaml\nsinks:\n  - id: SQL_EXEC\n    match:\n      node: CALL\n      api: { type: java.sql.Statement, method: executeQuery }\n      arg_index: 0\n```\n### Sanitizer\n```yaml\nsanitizers:\n  - id: ESCAPE_HTML\n    match: { node: CALL, api: { type: org.apache.commons.lang.StringEscapeUtils, method: escapeHtml } }\n    effect: neutralize\n    scope: { sinks: [XSS_SINK] }\n```\n### Propagation\n```yaml\npropagations:\n  - id: ARG_TO_PARAM\n    edge: arg->param | return | field-store | field-load | array | phi | alias\n    kind: taint | value\n```\n------\n\n            <doc/>\n            <IR_content>\n            以下是用户提交的中间表示，请进行转换：\n            ```yaml\nxcpgir_version: \"1.0\"\nrule:\n  id: \"tainted-sqli\"\n  kind: taint\n  severity: WARNING\n  message: \"Detected SQL statement that is tainted by `$EVENT` object.
